4612 — bodyweight standing around the world (wall supported)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4612 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing arm circle against a wall that mobilises the shoulders while keeping your back from compensating.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your back flat against a wall, feet slightly forward. Start with arms straight down at your sides, palms forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4612.json#/instructions/0, 4612.json#/instructions/1

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4612.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your back flat against a wall, feet slightly forward. 4612.json#/instructions/0
  2. Start with arms straight down at your sides, palms forward. 4612.json#/instructions/1
  3. Sweep your arms out to the sides and overhead in a wide arc, staying close to the wall. 4612.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower them back down the same way. 4612.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your ribs and head against the wall. 4612.json#/instructions/4

4613 — bodyweight standing military press (wall supported)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4613 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

An unloaded overhead press against a wall to groove a clean path and open the shoulders.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your back, head and hips against a wall, hands at shoulder height with elbows bent. Brace your abs so your lower back stays on the wall.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4613.json#/instructions/0, 4613.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Stand with your back, head and hips against a wall, hands at shoulder height with elbows bent.

Legs Feet

  2. Brace your abs so your lower back stays on the wall. 4613.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press your hands straight overhead until your arms are locked out. 4613.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower back to shoulder height with control. 4613.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your wrists stacked over your elbows. 4613.json#/instructions/4

Complete ordered source instructions

  5. Complete your reps, then switch legs. 4616.json#/instructions/4

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4617 after decompression) · Source record

4618 — band deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4618 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A hip hinge using a band under your feet for rising tension, working the glutes and hamstrings.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand on the band with feet hip-width apart and grab it with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4618.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand on the band with feet hip-width apart and grab it with both hands. 4618.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hinge at the hips with a flat back and slightly bent knees. 4618.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower your hands along your legs until you feel a hamstring stretch. 4618.json#/instructions/2
  4. Drive your hips forward to stand tall and squeeze your glutes. 4618.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep the band close to your body. 4618.json#/instructions/4
